 6130: 
 6131:     @staticmethod
 6132:     def _bind_criteria_to_commit(
 6133:         state: dict[str, Any], task_id: str, commit_hash: str
 6134:     ) -> None:
 6135:         evidence = state.get("criterion_evidence")
 6136:         if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
 6137:             return
 6138:         for item in evidence.values():
 6139:             if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("task_id") == task_id:
 6140:                 item["commit"] = commit_hash
 6141: 
 6142:     @staticmethod
 6143:     def _record_approved_criteria(
 6144:         state: dict[str, Any],
 6145:         plan: dict[str, Any],
 6146:         review: dict[str, Any],
 6147:         *,
 6148:         base_commit: str,
 6149:         candidate_id: str,
 6150:         review_ref: str,
 6151:         verification_ref: str | None,
 6152:     ) -> None:
 6153:         evidence = state.get("criterion_evidence")
 6154:         if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
 6155:             evidence = {}
 6156:         raw_owned = plan.get("global_criteria_ids", plan.get("global_criteria", []))
 6157:         owned = {
 6158:             item.get("id") if isinstance(item, dict) else item
 6159:             for item in raw_owned
 6160:             if isinstance(item, (dict, str))
 6161:         }
 6162:         passed = {
 6163:             item.get("criterion")
 6164:             for item in review.get("criteria", [])
 6165:             if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("passed") is True
 6166:         }
 6167:         for criterion_id in sorted(owned & passed):
 6168:             evidence[criterion_id] = {
 6169:                 "status": "PASS",
 6170:                 "task_id": plan.get("task_id"),
 6171:                 "base_commit": base_commit,
 6172:                 "candidate_id": candidate_id,
 6173:                 "review_ref": review_ref,
 6174:                 "verification_ref": verification_ref,
 6175:                 "task_scope": list(plan.get("allowed_files", [])),
 6176:             }
 6177:         state["criterion_evidence"] = evidence
 6178: 
 6179:     @staticmethod
 6180:     def _invalidate_affected_criteria(
 6181:         state: dict[str, Any], task: dict[str, Any]
 6182:     ) -> list[str]:
 6183:         evidence = state.get("criterion_evidence")
 6184:         if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
 6185:             return []
 6186:         task_id = task.get("id")
 6187:         task_criteria = set(task.get("global_criteria", []))
 6188:         task_scope = set(task.get("editable_scope", []))
 6189:         affected: list[str] = []
 6190:         for criterion_id, item in evidence.items():
 6191:             if not isinstance(item, dict):
 6192:                 continue
 6193:             prior_scope = set(item.get("task_scope", [])) if isinstance(item.get("task_scope"), list) else set()
 6194:             if (
 6195:                 criterion_id in task_criteria
 6196:                 or item.get("task_id") == task_id
 6197:                 or not task_criteria
 6198:                 or not prior_scope
 6199:                 or prior_scope & task_scope
 6200:             ):
 6201:                 item["status"] = "UNVERIFIED"
 6202:                 item.pop("commit", None)
 6203:                 affected.append(criterion_id)
 6204:         return sorted(set(affected))
 6205: 
 6206:     @staticmethod
 6207:     def _remaining_master_plan_gaps(
 6208:         master_plan: dict[str, Any], state: dict[str, Any]
 6209:     ) -> list[str]:
 6210:         evidence = state.get("criterion_evidence")
 6211:         if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
 6212:             evidence = {}
 6213:         committed = {
 6214:             item.get("commit")
 6215:             for item in state.get("completed_commits", [])
 6216:             if isinstance(item, dict)
 6217:         }
 6218:         return [
 6219:             criterion["id"]
 6220:             for criterion in master_plan["global_criteria"]
 6221:             if not isinstance(evidence.get(criterion["id"]), dict)
 6222:             or evidence[criterion["id"]].get("status") != "PASS"
 6223:             or evidence[criterion["id"]].get("commit") not in committed
 6224:             or not isinstance(evidence[criterion["id"]].get("task_scope"), list)
 6225:             or not evidence[criterion["id"]].get("task_scope")
 6226:             or not evidence[criterion["id"]].get("review_ref")
 6227:             or not evidence[criterion["id"]].get("verification_ref")
 6228:         ]
 6229: 
 6230:     @staticmethod
 6231:     def _durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
 6232:         values: list[int] = []
 6233:         for path in run_dir.glob("replan_reason_*.json"):
 6234:             match = re.fullmatch(r"replan_reason_([0-9]+)\.json", path.name)
 6235:             if match:
 6236:                 values.append(int(match.group(1)))
 6237:         reason_ref = state.get("replan_reason_ref")
 6238:         if isinstance(reason_ref, str):
 6239:             match = re.fullmatch(r"replan_reason_([0-9]+)\.json", reason_ref)
 6240:             if match:
 6241:                 values.append(int(match.group(1)))
 6242:         return max(values, default=0)
 6243: 
 6244:     def _persist_replan_reason(
 6245:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], reason: dict[str, Any]
 6246:     ) -> str:
 6247:         reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6248:         attempt = self._durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir, state) + 1
 6249:         if attempt > 2:
 6250:             block_reason = "exceptional replan budget exhausted; run is blocked"
 6251:             evidence_ref = "replan_blocked.json"
 6252:             atomic_json(
 6253:                 run_dir / evidence_ref,
 6254:                 {
 6255:                     "reason": block_reason,
 6256:                     "replan_attempts": attempt - 1,
 6257:                     "last_reason": reason,
 6258:                 },
 6259:             )
 6260:             self._save(
 6261:                 run_dir,
 6262:                 state,
 6263:                 "blocked",
 6264:                 block_reason=block_reason,
 6265:                 error=block_reason,
 6266:                 error_category="blocked",
 6267:                 replan_block_evidence=evidence_ref,
 6268:             )
 6269:             raise FactoryError(block_reason, "blocked")
 6270:         reason_ref = f"replan_reason_{attempt}.json"
 6271:         reason_path = run_dir / reason_ref
 6272:         if reason_path.exists():
 6273:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history is ambiguous", "blocked")
 6274:         atomic_json(reason_path, reason)
 6275:         self._save(run_dir, state, "planning", replan_reason_ref=reason_ref)
 6276:         return reason_ref
 6277: 
 6278:     def request_replan(self, identifier: str, reason: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6279:         """Accept only a verified structured strategic trigger for an Astra replan."""
 6280:         normalized_reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6281:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 6282:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
 6283:             run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 6284:             if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 6285:                 return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 6286:             if state.get("candidate_id") is not None or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
 6287:                 raise FactoryError("cannot replan with an ambiguous candidate or pending commit", "blocked")
 6288:             plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 6289:             reason_ref = self._persist_replan_reason(run_dir, state, normalized_reason)
 6290:             ledger = MemoryLedger(
 6291:                 run_dir,
 6292:                 identifier,
 6293:                 config.repo,
 6294:                 state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
 6295:             )
 6296:             self._perform_planning(
 6297:                 run_dir,
 6298:                 state,
 6299:                 config,
 6300:                 self._repo(config.repo),
 6301:                 ledger=ledger,
 6302:                 replan_reason=json.dumps(normalized_reason, sort_keys=True),
 6303:                 planning_repo=(
 6304:                     Path(state["worktree"])
 6305:                     if isinstance(state.get("worktree"), str)
 6306:                     and Path(state["worktree"]).is_dir()
 6307:                     else None
 6308:                 ),
 6309:             )
 6310:             state["replan_reason_ref"] = reason_ref
 6311:             atomic_json(run_dir / "run_state.json", state)
 6312:             return self._execute_locked(identifier)
 6313: 
 6314:     def _reconcile_pending_commit(
 6315:         self,
 6316:         identifier: str,
 6317:         run_dir: Path,
 6318:         state: dict[str, Any],
 6319:         config: Config,
 6320:         master_plan: dict[str, Any],
 6460:             "commit": commit_hash,
 6461:             "candidate_id": expected_candidate,
 6462:             "base_commit": base_commit,
 6463:         }
 6464:         if existing is None:
 6465:             completed_commits.append(completed_record)
 6466:         elif existing.get("commit") != commit_hash or existing.get("candidate_id") != expected_candidate:
 6467:             raise FactoryError("completed task history conflicts with pending commit", "blocked")
 6468:         state["completed_commits"] = completed_commits
 6469:         state["base_commit"] = commit_hash
 6470:         state["candidate_id"] = None
 6471:         state["pending_commit"] = None
 6472:         state["active_attempt_ref"] = None
 6473:         self._record_approved_criteria(
 6474:             state,
 6475:             task,
 6476:             review,
 6477:             base_commit=base_commit,
 6478:             candidate_id=expected_candidate,
 6479:             review_ref=str(state.get("review_ref")),
 6480:             verification_ref=state.get("verification_ref"),
 6481:         )
 6482:         self._record_committed_task_criteria(state, master_plan, task)
 6483:         self._bind_criteria_to_commit(state, task_id, commit_hash)
 6484:         self._save(
 6485:             run_dir,
 6486:             state,
 6487:             "planned",
 6488:             commit=commit_hash,
 6489:             current_task_id=task_id,
 6490:             completed_commits=completed_commits,
 6491:             criterion_evidence=state["criterion_evidence"],
 6492:             pending_commit=None,
 6493:             candidate_id=None,
 6494:             candidate_state_identity=None,
 6495:             active_attempt_ref=None,
 6496:         )
 6497:         return state
 6498: 
 6499:     def _validate_terminal_state(
 6500:         self,
 6501:         identifier: str,
 6502:         run_dir: Path,
 6503:         state: dict[str, Any],
 6504:         config: Config,
 6505:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6506:         """Revalidate a terminal snapshot before trusting its fast path."""
 6507:         def invalid(reason: str) -> None:
 6508:             self._save(
 6509:                 run_dir,
 6510:                 state,
 6511:                 "blocked",
 6512:                 block_reason=reason,
 6513:                 error=reason,
 6514:                 error_category="blocked",
 6515:             )
 6516:             raise FactoryError(reason, "blocked")
 6517: 
 6518:         try:
 6519:             plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 6520:             tasks = master_plan_tasks(plan)
 6521:             completed = state.get("completed_commits")
 6522:             if not isinstance(completed, list) or not completed:
 6523:                 invalid("terminal state has no completed commit history")
 6524:             task_ids: set[str] = set()
 6525:             plan_ids = {task["id"] for task in tasks}
 6526:             for record in completed:
 6527:                 if not isinstance(record, dict):
 6528:                     invalid("terminal state has an invalid completed commit record")
 6529:                 task_id = record.get("task_id")
 6530:                 commit = record.get("commit")
 6531:                 candidate_id = record.get("candidate_id")
 6532:                 base_commit = record.get("base_commit")
 6533:                 if (
 6534:                     not isinstance(task_id, str)
 6535:                     or task_id in task_ids
 6536:                     or not isinstance(commit, str)
 6537:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", commit)
 6538:                     or not isinstance(candidate_id, str)
 6539:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", candidate_id)
 6540:                     or not isinstance(base_commit, str)
 6541:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", base_commit)
 6542:                 ):
 6543:                     invalid("terminal state has an invalid or duplicate completed commit record")
 6544:                 if task_id not in plan_ids:
 6545:                     invalid("terminal state references a task absent from the master plan")
 6546:                 task_ids.add(task_id)
 6547:                 if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^").stdout.strip() != base_commit:
 6548:                     invalid("terminal state commit parent does not match its recorded base")
 6549:                 if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^{{tree}}").stdout.strip() != candidate_id:
 6550:                     invalid("terminal state commit tree does not match its recorded candidate")
 6551:             if task_ids != plan_ids:
 6552:                 invalid("terminal state is missing a committed master-plan task")
 6553:             if state.get("commit") != completed[-1].get("commit"):
 6554:                 invalid("terminal state latest commit is not bound to completed history")
 6555:             if state.get("current_task_id") is not None:
 6556:                 invalid("terminal state retains a current task")
 6557:             if any(state.get(key) is not None for key in ("candidate_id", "pending_commit", "active_attempt_ref")):
 6558:                 invalid("terminal state retains an ambiguous candidate or attempt")
 6559:             gaps = self._remaining_master_plan_gaps(plan, state)
 6560:             if gaps:
 6561:                 invalid(f"terminal state has incomplete global criterion evidence: {gaps}")
 6562:             evidence = state.get("criterion_evidence")
 6563:             if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
 6564:                 invalid("terminal state criterion evidence is missing")
 6565:             completed_by_commit = {
 6566:                 item["commit"]: item
 6567:                 for item in completed
 6568:                 if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("commit"), str)
 6569:             }
 6570:             for criterion_id, item in evidence.items():
 6571:                 if not isinstance(item, dict) or item.get("status") != "PASS":
 6572:                     continue
 6573:                 commit_record = completed_by_commit.get(item.get("commit"))
 6574:                 if commit_record is None or item.get("candidate_id") != commit_record.get("candidate_id"):
 6575:                     invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has stale commit lineage")
 6576:                 for field in ("review_ref", "verification_ref"):
 6577:                     reference = item.get(field)
 6578:                     if not isinstance(reference, str) or not reference:
 6579:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has no {field}")
 6580:                     artifact = (run_dir / reference).resolve()
 6581:                     try:
 6582:                         artifact.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6583:                     except ValueError:
 6584:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has an escaping {field}")
 6585:                     if not artifact.is_file():
 6586:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references missing {field}")
 6587:                     artifact_value = read_json(artifact)
 6588:                     if field == "review_ref" and (
 6589:                         not isinstance(artifact_value, dict) or artifact_value.get("decision") != "APPROVE"
 6590:                     ):
 6591:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references a non-approval review")
 6592:                     if field == "review_ref" and (
 6593:                         artifact_value.get("base_commit") != item.get("base_commit")
 6594:                         or artifact_value.get("candidate_id") != item.get("candidate_id")
 6595:                     ):
 6596:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} review identity is stale")
 6597:                     if field == "verification_ref" and (
 6598:                         not isinstance(artifact_value, dict) or artifact_value.get("passed") is not True
 6599:                     ):
 6600:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references failed verification")
 6601:             ledger = MemoryLedger(
 6602:                 run_dir,
 6603:                 identifier,
 6604:                 config.repo,
 6605:                 state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
 6606:             )
 6607:             if any(item.get("status") in {"open", "regressed"} for item in ledger.issues.values()):
 6608:                 invalid("terminal state has unresolved ledger findings")
 6609:         except FactoryError as exc:
 6610:             if state.get("phase") != "blocked":
 6611:                 invalid(f"terminal state validation failed: {exc}")
 6612:             raise
 6613:         except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeError) as exc:
 6614:             invalid(f"terminal state evidence is invalid: {exc}")
 6615:         return state

         "truncation_notice": (

             if truncated
             else None
         ),
         "safety_boundary_note": (
             "Historical context only. Do not interpret prior issues as permission to expand allowed_files or redesign architecture."
         ),
     }
     return context
 
 
 class FactoryError(RuntimeError):
     """Expected factory failure with a stable category."""
 
     def __init__(self, message: str, category: str = "failed") -> None:
         super().__init__(message)
         self.category = category
 
 
+class ReplanRequested(FactoryError):

+REPLAN_REASON_CODES = {

+    "PLAN_EXHAUSTED_OBJECTIVE_INCOMPLETE",

 def now_iso() -> str:
     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
 
 
 def run_id() -> str:
     stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
     return f"{stamp}-{os.getpid()}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
 
 
 def atomic_json(path: Path, value: Any) -> None:
     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
     fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
     try:
         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
             json.dump(value, handle, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
             handle.write("\n")
             handle.flush()
             os.fsync(handle.fileno())
         os.replace(temporary, path)
         os.chmod(path, 0o600)
     finally:
         if os.path.exists(temporary):
             os.unlink(temporary)

     lock_path = run_dir / "run.lock"

 def parse_json_object(text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
     candidate = text.strip()
     # Match the complete fence contents. A non-greedy brace match truncates
     # valid nested objects such as Sol review criteria at their first `}`.
     fenced = re.search(r"```(?:json)?\s*(.*?)\s*```", candidate, re.S | re.I)
     if fenced:
         candidate = fenced.group(1).strip()
     else:
         first, last = candidate.find("{"), candidate.rfind("}")
         if first >= 0 and last > first:
             candidate = candidate[first : last + 1]
     try:
         value = json.loads(candidate)
     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
         raise FactoryError(f"model did not return valid JSON: {exc}", "contract") from exc
     if not isinstance(value, dict):
         raise FactoryError("model response must be a JSON object", "contract")
     return value
 
 
 def require_keys(value: dict[str, Any], keys: set[str], label: str) -> None:
     missing = sorted(keys - value.keys())
     extra = sorted(value.keys() - keys)
     if missing or extra:
         raise FactoryError(
             f"{label} schema mismatch; missing={missing}, extra={extra}", "contract"
         )
 
 
+def validate_replan_reason(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {"code", "evidence"}:
+        raise FactoryError(
+            "exceptional replan reason must contain exactly code and evidence",
+            "contract",
+        )
+    code = value.get("code")
+    evidence = value.get("evidence")
+    if code not in REPLAN_REASON_CODES:
+        raise FactoryError("exceptional replan reason has an unsupported code", "contract")
+    if not isinstance(evidence, dict) or not evidence:
+        raise FactoryError("exceptional replan reason evidence must be a non-empty object", "contract")
+    return {"code": code, "evidence": evidence}
+
+
 def string_list(value: Any, field: str, *, allow_empty: bool = False) -> list[str]:
     if not isinstance(value, list) or (not value and not allow_empty):
         raise FactoryError(f"{field} must be a non-empty JSON array", "contract")
     if not all(isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in value):
         raise FactoryError(f"{field} must contain only non-empty strings", "contract")
     return [item.strip() for item in value]
 
 
 def normalize_acceptance_criteria(value: Any, task_id: str) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
     if not isinstance(value, list) or not value:
         raise FactoryError(f"task {task_id} acceptance_criteria must be a non-empty array", "contract")
     normalized: list[dict[str, str]] = []
     seen: set[str] = set()
     for index, item in enumerate(value, start=1):
         if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip():
             criterion = {"id": f"{task_id}.AC{index}", "statement": item.strip()}
         elif isinstance(item, dict):
             require_keys(item, {"id", "statement"}, f"task {task_id} acceptance criterion")
             criterion_id = _validate_plan_identifier(item.get("id"), f"task {task_id} acceptance criterion id")
             statement = item.get("statement")
             if not isinstance(statement, str) or not statement.strip():
                 raise FactoryError(f"task {task_id} acceptance criterion statement must be non-empty", "contract")
             criterion = {"id": criterion_id, "statement": statement.strip()}
         else:
             raise FactoryError(f"task {task_id} acceptance_criteria has an invalid entry", "contract")
         if criterion["id"] in seen:
             raise FactoryError(f"task {task_id} has duplicate acceptance criterion id", "contract")
         seen.add(criterion["id"])
         normalized.append(criterion)
     return normalized

 def failure_signature(failure: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
     """Return the small deterministic identity used by the stagnation rule."""
     return (
         failure.get("source"),
         failure.get("code") or failure.get("error_category"),
         tuple(sorted(str(item) for item in failure.get("criterion_ids", []))),
         failure.get("returncode"),
     )
 
 
 def is_stagnant(
     previous_candidate_id: str | None,
     current_candidate_id: str | None,
     previous_failure: dict[str, Any],
     current_failure: dict[str, Any],
 ) -> bool:
     return (
         previous_candidate_id is not None
         and previous_candidate_id == current_candidate_id
         and failure_signature(previous_failure) == failure_signature(current_failure)
     )

 def _replace_candidate_file(
     source: Path,
     target: Path,
     *,
     race_safe: bool,
     expected_target: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
     target_fd: int | None = None,

     if expected_target is not None:

             data = os.read(target_fd, max(1, os.fstat(target_fd).st_size))
             current_target = {
                 "kind": "file",
                 "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),
                 "size": len(data),
             }
         elif target.is_file():
             data = target.read_bytes()
             current_target = {
                 "kind": "file",
                 "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),
                 "size": len(data),
             }

+        try:
+            # NTFS hard-link creation is atomic and fails if the destination
+            # appeared after the CAS check; the staged source stays intact
+            # until the link succeeds.
+            os.link(source, target)
+        except FileExistsError as exc:
+            raise OSError("candidate target appeared before atomic creation") from exc
+        source.unlink()
+        return
     if not race_safe or os.name != "nt":
         os.replace(source, target)

     import ctypes
     import ctypes.wintypes as wintypes
 
     replace_file = ctypes.windll.kernel32.ReplaceFileW
     replace_file.argtypes = [
         wintypes.LPCWSTR,
         wintypes.LPCWSTR,
         wintypes.LPCWSTR,
         wintypes.DWORD,
         wintypes.LPVOID,
         wintypes.LPVOID,
     ]
     replace_file.restype = wintypes.BOOL
     if not replace_file(str(target), str(source), None, 0, None, None):
         raise OSError(ctypes.get_last_error(), "could not replace candidate target")

+    @staticmethod
+    def _durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
+        values: list[int] = []
+        for path in run_dir.glob("replan_reason_*.json"):
+            match = re.fullmatch(r"replan_reason_([0-9]+)\.json", path.name)
+            if match:
+                values.append(int(match.group(1)))
+        reason_ref = state.get("replan_reason_ref")
+        if isinstance(reason_ref, str):
+            match = re.fullmatch(r"replan_reason_([0-9]+)\.json", reason_ref)
+            if match:
+                values.append(int(match.group(1)))
+        return max(values, default=0)
+
+    def _persist_replan_reason(
+        self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], reason: dict[str, Any]
+    ) -> str:
+        reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
+        attempt = self._durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir, state) + 1
+        if attempt > 2:
+            block_reason = "exceptional replan budget exhausted; run is blocked"
+            evidence_ref = "replan_blocked.json"
+            atomic_json(
+                run_dir / evidence_ref,
+                {
+                    "reason": block_reason,
+                    "replan_attempts": attempt - 1,
+                    "last_reason": reason,
+                },
+            )
+            self._save(
+                run_dir,
+                state,
+                "blocked",
+                block_reason=block_reason,
+                error=block_reason,
+                error_category="blocked",
+                replan_block_evidence=evidence_ref,
+            )
+            raise FactoryError(block_reason, "blocked")
+        reason_ref = f"replan_reason_{attempt}.json"
+        reason_path = run_dir / reason_ref
+        if reason_path.exists():
+            raise FactoryError("replan reason history is ambiguous", "blocked")
+        atomic_json(reason_path, reason)
+        self._save(run_dir, state, "planning", replan_reason_ref=reason_ref)
+        return reason_ref

+    def request_replan(self, identifier: str, reason: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Accept only a verified structured strategic trigger for an Astra replan."""
+        normalized_reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
+        run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
+        with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
+            run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
+            if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
+                return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
+            if state.get("candidate_id") is not None or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
+                raise FactoryError("cannot replan with an ambiguous candidate or pending commit", "blocked")
+            plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
+            reason_ref = self._persist_replan_reason(run_dir, state, normalized_reason)
+            ledger = MemoryLedger(
+                run_dir,
+                identifier,
+                config.repo,
+                state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
+            )
+            self._perform_planning(
+                run_dir,
+                state,
+                config,
+                self._repo(config.repo),
+                ledger=ledger,
+                replan_reason=json.dumps(normalized_reason, sort_keys=True),
+                planning_repo=(
+                    Path(state["worktree"])
+                    if isinstance(state.get("worktree"), str)
+                    and Path(state["worktree"]).is_dir()
+                    else None
+                ),
+            )
+            state["replan_reason_ref"] = reason_ref
+            atomic_json(run_dir / "run_state.json", state)
+            return self._execute_locked(identifier)

+    def _validate_terminal_state(
+        self,
+        identifier: str,
+        run_dir: Path,
+        state: dict[str, Any],
+        config: Config,
+    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Revalidate a terminal snapshot before trusting its fast path."""
+        def invalid(reason: str) -> None:
+            self._save(
+                run_dir,
+                state,
+                "blocked",
+                block_reason=reason,
+                error=reason,
+                error_category="blocked",
+            )
+            raise FactoryError(reason, "blocked")
+
+        try:
+            plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
+            tasks = master_plan_tasks(plan)
+            completed = state.get("completed_commits")
+            if not isinstance(completed, list) or not completed:
+                invalid("terminal state has no completed commit history")
+            task_ids: set[str] = set()
+            plan_ids = {task["id"] for task in tasks}
+            for record in completed:
+                if not isinstance(record, dict):
+                    invalid("terminal state has an invalid completed commit record")
+                task_id = record.get("task_id")
+                commit = record.get("commit")
+                candidate_id = record.get("candidate_id")
+                base_commit = record.get("base_commit")
+                if (
+                    not isinstance(task_id, str)
+                    or task_id in task_ids
+                    or not isinstance(commit, str)
+                    or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", commit)
+                    or not isinstance(candidate_id, str)
+                    or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", candidate_id)
+                    or not isinstance(base_commit, str)
+                    or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", base_commit)
+                ):
+                    invalid("terminal state has an invalid or duplicate completed commit record")
+                if task_id not in plan_ids:
+                    invalid("terminal state references a task absent from the master plan")
+                task_ids.add(task_id)
+                if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^").stdout.strip() != base_commit:
+                    invalid("terminal state commit parent does not match its recorded base")
+                if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^{{tree}}").stdout.strip() != candidate_id:
+                    invalid("terminal state commit tree does not match its recorded candidate")
+            if task_ids != plan_ids:
+                invalid("terminal state is missing a committed master-plan task")
+            if state.get("commit") != completed[-1].get("commit"):
+                invalid("terminal state latest commit is not bound to completed history")
+            if state.get("current_task_id") is not None:
+                invalid("terminal state retains a current task")
+            if any(state.get(key) is not None for key in ("candidate_id", "pending_commit", "active_attempt_ref")):
+                invalid("terminal state retains an ambiguous candidate or attempt")
+            gaps = self._remaining_master_plan_gaps(plan, state)
+            if gaps:
+                invalid(f"terminal state has incomplete global criterion evidence: {gaps}")
+            evidence = state.get("criterion_evidence")
+            if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
+                invalid("terminal state criterion evidence is missing")
+            completed_by_commit = {
+                item["commit"]: item
+                for item in completed
+                if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("commit"), str)
+            }
+            for criterion_id, item in evidence.items():
+                if not isinstance(item, dict) or item.get("status") != "PASS":
+                    continue
+                commit_record = completed_by_commit.get(item.get("commit"))
+                if commit_record is None or item.get("candidate_id") != commit_record.get("candidate_id"):
+                    invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has stale commit lineage")
+                for field in ("review_ref", "verification_ref"):
+                    reference = item.get(field)
+                    if not isinstance(reference, str) or not reference:
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has no {field}")
+                    artifact = (run_dir / reference).resolve()
+                    try:
+                        artifact.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
+                    except ValueError:
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has an escaping {field}")
+                    if not artifact.is_file():
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references missing {field}")
+                    artifact_value = read_json(artifact)
+                    if field == "review_ref" and (
+                        not isinstance(artifact_value, dict) or artifact_value.get("decision") != "APPROVE"
+                    ):
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references a non-approval review")
+                    if field == "review_ref" and (
+                        artifact_value.get("base_commit") != item.get("base_commit")
+                        or artifact_value.get("candidate_id") != item.get("candidate_id")
+                    ):
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} review identity is stale")
+                    if field == "verification_ref" and (
+                        not isinstance(artifact_value, dict) or artifact_value.get("passed") is not True
+                    ):
+                        invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references failed verification")
+            ledger = MemoryLedger(
+                run_dir,
+                identifier,
+                config.repo,
+                state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
+            )
+            if any(item.get("status") in {"open", "regressed"} for item in ledger.issues.values()):
+                invalid("terminal state has unresolved ledger findings")
+        except FactoryError as exc:
+            if state.get("phase") != "blocked":
+                invalid(f"terminal state validation failed: {exc}")
+            raise
+        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeError) as exc:
+            invalid(f"terminal state evidence is invalid: {exc}")
+        return state
+

 3519:     @staticmethod
 3520:     def _sandbox_host_environment() -> dict[str, str]:
 3521:         # Windows environment names are case-insensitive.  Preserve the
 3522:         # inherited spelling while matching the minimal launcher allowlist in
 3523:         # a case-insensitive way; omitting SYSTEMROOT makes legacy
 3524:         # WindowsPowerShell fail before WindowsSandbox.exe is started.
 3525:         allowed = {"SYSTEMROOT", "WINDIR", "PATH", "TEMP", "TMP"}
 3526:         return {
 3527:             key: value
 3528:             for key, value in os.environ.items()
 3529:             if key.upper() in allowed and isinstance(value, str) and "\x00" not in value
 3530:         }

 3532:     @staticmethod
 3533:     def _read_windows_oauth_credential() -> bytes:
 3534:         """Read the approved host OAuth credential without exposing its value."""
 3535:         if os.name != "nt":
 3536:             raise OSError("Windows Credential Manager is unavailable on this platform")
 3537:         import ctypes
 3538:         import ctypes.wintypes as wintypes
 3539: 
 3540:         class Credential(ctypes.Structure):
 3541:             pass
 3542: 
 3543:         credential_pointer = ctypes.POINTER(Credential)
 3544:         Credential._fields_ = [
 3545:             ("flags", wintypes.DWORD),
 3546:             ("type", wintypes.DWORD),
 3547:             ("target_name", wintypes.LPWSTR),
 3548:             ("comment", wintypes.LPWSTR),
 3549:             ("last_written", wintypes.FILETIME),
 3550:             ("credential_blob_size", wintypes.DWORD),
 3551:             ("credential_blob", ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_ubyte)),
 3552:             ("persist", wintypes.DWORD),
 3553:             ("attribute_count", wintypes.DWORD),
 3554:             ("attributes", ctypes.c_void_p),
 3555:             ("target_alias", wintypes.LPWSTR),
 3556:             ("user_name", wintypes.LPWSTR),
 3557:         ]
 3558:         read = ctypes.windll.advapi32.CredReadW
 3559:         read.argtypes = [wintypes.LPWSTR, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(credential_pointer)]
 3560:         read.restype = wintypes.BOOL
 3561:         free = ctypes.windll.advapi32.CredFree
 3562:         free.argtypes = [credential_pointer]
 3563:         credential = credential_pointer()
 3564:         if not read("gemini:antigravity", 1, 0, ctypes.byref(credential)):
 3565:             raise OSError(ctypes.get_last_error(), "approved host AGY OAuth credential is unavailable")

 3567:             size = int(credential.contents.credential_blob_size)
 3568:             if size <= 0 or size > 64 * 1024:
 3569:                 raise OSError("approved host AGY OAuth credential has an invalid size")
 3570:             data = ctypes.string_at(credential.contents.credential_blob, size)
 3571:         finally:
 3572:             free(credential)
 3573:         try:
 3574:             payload = json.loads(data.decode("utf-8"))
 3575:         except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
 3576:             raise OSError("approved host AGY OAuth credential is malformed") from exc
 3577:         token = payload.get("token") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None

 3580:             or not isinstance(token.get("access_token"), str)

 3584:         ):

 5521:     def _changed_files(self, worktree: Path) -> list[str]:
 5522:         tracked = git(worktree, "diff", "--name-only", "HEAD", "--").stdout.splitlines()
 5523:         untracked = git(worktree, "ls-files", "--others", "--exclude-standard").stdout.splitlines()

 5526:             "ls-files",
 5527:             "--others",
 5528:             "--ignored",

 5533:     def _scope_check(self, worktree: Path, allowed: list[str], label: str, run_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
 5534:         # Revalidate after every builder/test boundary. A builder can replace a
 5535:         # previously safe file with a symlink before staging.
 5536:         ensure_paths_confined(worktree, allowed)
 5537:         changed = self._changed_files(worktree)
 5538:         outside = sorted(set(changed) - set(allowed))
 5539:         evidence = {"label": label, "changed_files": changed, "allowed_files": allowed, "outside_scope": outside}
 5540:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"scope_{label}.json", evidence)
 5541:         if outside:
 5542:             raise FactoryError(f"scope violation after {label}: {outside}", "scope")
 5543:         return changed

 5548:     def _detect_test(self, worktree: Path) -> str | None:
 5549:         package = worktree / "package.json"
 5550:         if package.is_file():
 5551:             try:
 5552:                 data = read_json(package)
 5553:                 if isinstance(data.get("scripts", {}).get("test"), str):
 5554:                     return "npm test"
 5555:             except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
 5556:                 pass
 5557:         if (worktree / "pytest.ini").is_file() or (worktree / "pyproject.toml").is_file() or (worktree / "tests").is_dir():

 5560:             return "cargo test"

 5576:             }

 5578:             raise FactoryError("no test command supplied or safely auto-detected", "test")

# F002: same-content creation race must preserve the host writer.
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
    root = Path(td)
    candidate = root / 'candidate'; candidate.mkdir()
    subprocess.run(['git','init','-q'], cwd=candidate, check=True)
    subprocess.run(['git','config','user.email','probe@example.invalid'], cwd=candidate, check=True)
    subprocess.run(['git','config','user.name','Probe'], cwd=candidate, check=True)
    (candidate/'seed.py').write_text('seed = 1\\n', encoding='utf-8')
    subprocess.run(['git','add','seed.py'], cwd=candidate, check=True)
    subprocess.run(['git','commit','-qm','seed'], cwd=candidate, check=True)
    mirror = root / 'mirror'
    f.AgyBuilder._copy_sandbox_candidate(candidate, mirror)
    before = f._snapshot_probe_tree(mirror)
    baseline = f.AgyBuilder._candidate_baseline_identity(candidate)
    wanted = b'created = True\\n'
    (mirror/'new.py').write_bytes(wanted)
    original = f._replace_candidate_file
    raced = {'done': False}
    def same_content_host_race(source, target, **kwargs):
        if not raced['done']:
            raced['done'] = True
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_bytes(source.read_bytes())
        return original(source, target, **kwargs)
    try:
        with patch.object(f, '_replace_candidate_file', side_effect=same_content_host_race):
            f.AgyBuilder._sync_sandbox_candidate(candidate, mirror, ['new.py'], before, baseline,
                stdout_path=root/'stdout', stderr_path=root/'stderr')
        outcome = 'unexpected_success'
    except f.BuilderProcessError as exc:
        outcome = exc.failure_code
    results['F002_same_content_race'] = {
        'outcome': outcome,
        'host_file_exists_after_rejection': (candidate/'new.py').exists(),
        'host_file_bytes_preserved': (candidate/'new.py').read_bytes() == wanted if (candidate/'new.py').exists() else False,
    }

# F003: repeated crash after Astra returns can replay the same reserved reason forever.
case = FactoryIntegrationTests(methodName='test_replan_exhaustion_is_durable_blocked_without_refilling_astra_budget')
case.setUp()
try:
    roles, builder = FakeRoles(), FakeBuilder()
    engine = f.FactoryEngine(state_root=case.state_root, role_client=roles, builder=builder)
    identifier = engine.plan(case.config())
    original_call = roles.call
    def astra_returns_then_crash(**kwargs):
        value = original_call(**kwargs)
        if kwargs.get('role') == 'Astra Planner' and roles.astra_invocation_count > 1:
            raise KeyboardInterrupt('lost Astra response after invocation')
        return value
    snapshots=[]
    with patch.object(f.FactoryEngine, '_record_approved_criteria', return_value=None), patch.object(roles, 'call', side_effect=astra_returns_then_crash):
        for _ in range(3):
            try: engine.execute(identifier)
            except KeyboardInterrupt: pass
            state=engine.status(identifier)
            snapshots.append({'phase':state['phase'], 'state_astra_count':state['astra_invocation_count'], 'role_astra_count':roles.astra_invocation_count,
                              'reason_files':sorted(p.name for p in (case.state_root/identifier).glob('replan_reason_*.json'))})
    results['F003_replayed_reserved_reason'] = snapshots
finally:
    case.tearDown()

finally:
    case.tearDown()
